lastTick исключает двойной шаг; освобождение journeyReleasedAt/agenda.due исключает второе личное действие в тике прибытия. Новый этап ещё не начисляет все нужды специальных ролей — это .2b.3. Off сохраняет/приостанавливает новыеjourneys; прежние записи сarrival и безversion завершаются прежним способом. Events≤160/knowledge≤8, действительные witnesses/knownBy даже независимо отbackground-towns. Сцена/Мир показывают нового путника только при реальном местном теле, не дают удалённого слежения; renderer чистый/без новых объектов.

### Одна деятельность NPC (B-01.2b.3a)

npc-controller.v1 / npc-control выбирает владельца деятельности: герой → отряд → служба → груз → общественное путешествие → переселение → личные дела. Опора — существующие канонические записи с постоянным personID. Проигравшее дело не удаляется без исхода. WeakMap индексирует только claims по тику, heroID, ссылкам и размерам четырёх таблиц. Физические alive/status и migration/arrivals читаются заново. Индекс не сериализуется и не зависит от карты героя.

Необязательный actionControl{tick,owner,phases[]} хранит последний активный тик и до четырёх фиксированных фаз. npcAction запрещает повтор фазы и другую роль в том же тике. У отряда upkeep/social/goal — части одного контроллера. Наблюдение, пассивный урон и получение подарка не считаются новой самостоятельной работой. Начало рейса, путешествия, службы или семейного исхода помечается depart. После уже выполненной работы новая отправка ждёт следующего тика.

People обслуживает personal/migration. Рекрутинг явно сбрасывает личную задачу; расставание возвращает управление без второго хода в тот же тик. Maintenance переселения не переписывает дело человека, занятого отрядом или другой ролью. Новый груз или путь с несогласованным физическим положением завершается остатком груза либо остановкой в настоящем месте. Возобновившийся legacy перевозчик освобождает жилое представление и запись population. Смерть такого возчика оставляет один конечный cache вместо доставки мёртвым персонажем.

Выключение сохраняет actionControl и возвращает прежнее расписание без новых записей. Отсутствие поля в v1 означает ещё не выполненный ход, а не необходимость сбросить прогресс. Нужды специальных ролей арбитр сам не начисляет: еда, сон и лечение остаются подэтапом .3b.

### Нужды физического путника и перевозчика (B-01.2b.3b.1)

npc-road-needs.v1 / npc-road-needs использует существующий personNeeds через необязательный адаптер источника еды, лечения и записи смерти. Без адаптера прежнее поведение жителей и мигрантов сохраняется. Обновление раз в8тиков; roadNeeds{tick,blocked,activity} хранит один результат и защищает повтор в том же тике. Сон удерживает настоящее area/x и remaining до пробуждения; еда и аптечка занимают один ход. Предел активного пути проверяется до нужд, поэтому спящий не обходит бюджет720.

Новая общественная отправка пополняет личный roadSupplies до двух единиц еды из реальной кладовой, сохраняя уже имевшийся запас. Для раненого или больного возможны до двух лекарств из доступного избытка, с двумя оставшимися в поселении. Ретроспективного изъятия для уже назначенного пути нет. Общая кладовая выдаёт дорожную помощь по существующей модели коллективных припасов, новый кошелёк или торговая цена не вводятся.

Сначала расходуется личный запас, затем конечный груз перевозчика. Одна еда снимает48голода; две единицы лекарства дают до25здоровья и могут снять болезнь после восстановления85%. Смешанный личный и перевозимый запас складывается только для реального списания, не клонируется. При наличии местной civic еды действует прежний fallback personNeeds, включая прежнюю плату из имеющихся накоплений. Сбор растений без действия не добавлен.

Голод может убить на настоящей дороге. Базовая нужда оставляет одну могилу; контроллер завершает путь и кладёт оставшийся конечный груз один раз, не доставляя дубль. Смерть хранит настоящую area, свидетелей и knownBy; удалённая запись не становится дневником героя. Краткие сведения о сне/еде остаются в ограниченной памяти самого NPC и видны герою только на общей сцене. Рисование использует состояние сна, не меняет симуляцию.

Выключение адаптера или npc-needs сохраняет запас и нужды, снимая дорожное влияние. Supply-off не тратит конечный груз; supply-off также приостанавливает сами грузовые рейсы по прежнему контракту. Абстрактные legacy arrivals с area=null, стража и человеческий отряд не объявлены обслуженными этим адаптером. Их оставшиеся части — .3b.2/3.

### Стража и личный запас после поручения (B-01.2b.3b.2)

npc-watch-needs.v1 / npc-watch-needs добавляет отдельный адаптер personNeeds под orders-контроллером. Новое поручение выдаёт до2еды и2лекарств из действительной исходной кладовой, оставляя два лекарства поселению. Уже имеющийся запас не пополняется сверх цели, старым поручениям ничего не дорисовывается. watchNeeds{tick,blocked,activity,retreat} — один сохранённый результат; метаболизм обновляется раз в8тиков, повтор обработчика не тратит вторую аптечку.

В дороге сон удерживает оставшееся время поручения. При вступлении в схватку первый боевой ход начинается следующим тиком. В бою еда и лечение занимают ход стража, после чего тот же живой враг действительно отвечает через enemyReply. Сон и разговор не блокируют вражеский бой. При силах<25, прежнем сне либо здоровье<30% без достаточной аптечки страж выбирает возвращение: делает один отход от врага и получает ответный удар. Этот удар может убить; бессмертного бесплатного отступления нет. Общие тактики, мутации, эффекты и регенерация врага продолжают применяться. При уже выполненной цели лишняя аптечка не расходуется.

Смерть закрывает одно orders и оставляет одну могилу, не присваивает герою боевой опыт. Локальные записи отправки, лечения, отступления, возвращения и смерти получают area/id/witnesses/knownBy; реальная победа NPC в engine использует ту же область знания. Невидимая победа не становится дневником героя. Идущая или возвращающаяся стража имеет одно видимое тело в действительной сцене; член отряда не дублируется как старый страж. Это не переделка прежнего таймерного межсценового маршрута orders в новый дорожный планировщик.

npc-personal-supply.v1 / npc-personal-supplies отдельно разрешает пользоваться своим roadSupplies после смены роли независимо от migrations. Общее consumeCarriedSupplies атомарно проверяет количество, списывает личное раньше перевозимого и не создаёт остаток из воздуха. Миграционный cargo сохраняет собственный контракт; skipPersonal предотвращает вторую попытку списания того же личного запаса. При supply-off новая личная политика не расходует имущество. Выключение каждого нового модуля сохраняет данные и возвращает прежнее поведение соответствующей роли.


### U-05: portrait frame and input boundary (stage65)
The game-shell contains header, scene, diary and dialogs. Available dynamic viewport minus safe-area determines height; width=min(available width,480px,height*9/16). Container queries select compact layouts without changing simulation coordinates, RNG or saves. Caption slots have stable height, fonts at least10px; dialogs scroll within the frame. Large money is abbreviated visually with exact amount in title/sheet. Public build explicitly includes both stylesheets and verifies referenced asset checksums.
A primary pointer must start and finish inside the actual game frame, outside interactive controls. Crossing its boundary cancels even below the normal drag threshold; window/visual viewport/root resize cancels hold and pending succession. Outer fields do not tick or postpone autoplay. Scroll does not tick; hold has no release tick. A terminal successor still requires its own fresh explicit press, with keyboard activation preserved.


### npc-party-needs.v1 (stage66)
The party owner retains exclusive upkeep control. Human needs update once per8ticks; a saved partyNeeds {tick,activity} also guards repeats when npc-control is disabled. No city or migration food/medicine is used implicitly. Own stock follows npc-personal-supplies and supply switches. One already-paid ration (made from4 food) or4 civic food feeds the hero plus at most3 humans; pets keep their former care rules. A passenger may share a real carried ration while sailing, without changing voyage progress.
Hunger≥65 influences the real food plan; energy<25, sleeping or health<45% influences the existing rest plan. Forecasts reserve24+ceil(next-route ticks*.1), capped90, human energy and55%health. Rest waits for90energy/no sleep/85%health as well as the hero. Human health is canonical and projects into companion HP; combat injury and rest continue to update the same person. Camp-life-off does not disable the new needs recovery.
During active combat upkeep only advances passive needs and starvation; it cannot also consume food/medicine. A human busy with care on this tick, sleeping or under25energy adds no attack/healing/route-strength assistance; attack facts match actual availability. Hostile replies retain existing combat rules. Starvation removes one companion, one stress/loss record, and one local grave. During transit the dead person records actual from/to/mode/tick with area=null, without inventing a source-city corpse or remote public death; no guaranteed later grave/revival mechanic is claimed. Disabling the module preserves its data and former behavior.


### actor-atlas.v1 and return supplies — stage70, OBS-01.2c.1

World area discovered/visited flags are historical compatibility projections, not a second adventurer's personal knowledge. Once observation can have multiple people, each detailed actor owns atlas.known/visited/roads/visits. The first current legacy body captures its existing visible map without losing progress; an already dormant body uses its own recorded places and NPC navigation, without inventing an undocumented distant rumour. Before a new birth the old map is captured; the new person learns its actual start and adjacent routes. An old single-person world keeps its original map until this handoff. This is mandatory identity knowledge, not an optional content switch.

Planning services/frontiers/work/quests, knownRoute and the map/family UI use that person's atlas. A known destination alone grants no unknown intervening road; remote new links between known places do not silently become known. Actual local observation notices adjacent routes before the actor's action, without XP/RNG or knowledge of distant changes. Physical generation, objects, links and world history remain shared. Actual first personal entry grants discovery XP; selecting the same body and revisiting do not. Optional story discovery sources reveal to their acting person. Full ordinary-NPC adoption/selection UI remains .2c.2.

Opened roadSupplies.food belongs to the same canonical person as inventory. A returning hero can plan and consume a meal from these real portions, first spending opened stock and opening a sealed ration only for a necessary shortfall. One funded portion covers the hero, then up to three funded human portions; unfunded humans receive neither hunger relief nor meal loyalty. A partial meal does not manufacture a full pack. Previously sealed whole-pack meals keep their four-serving behavior; supply-off spends neither form. Shipboard party meals use the same transfer. UI shows opened portions separately. Existing finite NPC medicine remains NPC-owned, with no invented potion conversion.

Legacy gift receipts now append recipient person-ID. Existing numeric generation receipts are still checked against the body's stable actorGeneration. Observation sequence or a compatibility generation change cannot collect again as the same person. Normal original item/coin transfers remain canonical and one-time.

### actor-phase.v1 — stage69, OBS-01.2b

advanceWorld alone advances the shared clock, once. Continuing detailed actors each run the existing autonomous planner through an actorView in stable actorGeneration/person-ID order. Views share world objects, scalar writes and RNG, but expose their own hero, generation and journal; they cannot replace the real observer or advance world time. Views and caches are not serialized. actorStep plus mandatory body ownership prevent a second action, including when optional npc-control is disabled. Retired NPC-mode actors keep their existing NPC controller. External person-record deltas are reconciled before actions; the body projection is synchronized after them.

Parties belong to their actual continuing leader, regardless of observation. Local contacts, event witnesses, freight sightings and patrol pauses use physical participants rather than only the selected hero. Background action facts use real person IDs, and other detailed bodies are drawn once only in the same actual scene; redraw does not step simulation. Each actor has a bounded private journal; only locally observable background actions enter the selected journal. Legacy root journal remains the selected journal projection and is saved once.

Internal createNextStory({allowLiving:true}) releases a living adventurer without ending its plan or creating an NPC job. Internal observeDetailedActor changes an existing living detailed target with no tick, birth, healing, resource reset or generation reuse. Neither is exposed as free living-selection UI yet: per-person atlas knowledge, ordinary-NPC adoption, opened food on return and person-keyed gift recipients remain OBS-01.2c. Death of one actor does not stop the world or the others and never creates a successor.

### observation.v1 — first foundation (stage67, OBS-01.1)
advanceWorld/step advances one shared tick, invokes the existing canonical world controllers, and invokes actObserved only for an alive non-ended body. It never creates a successor. createNextStory/startNextLife explicitly create once at the same tick. observation selects person-ID and sequence; actorStates holds the detailed canonical state and hero references its selected entry. serialize writes the current state once in its legacy hero slot, writes other actor states separately, and restore reattaches the reference without a world step. Manual reassignment of a test actor ID must reinitialize its observation identity.
A real death archives once, releases party ownership, transfers original belongings into one cache, then clears the body's active money/items/supplies/party. Legacy death migration retains person/tick/archive/graves and removes old item copies already represented in its existing heirloom. Death age/duration are fixed at endedAt; world date continues. NPC needs/work/transport/events continue through the old world controllers; unrelated module switches still apply. Afterlife journal observations do not enter the dead person's memory/biography or become their speech.
Ordinary UI taps/hold/autoplay/offline advance the world even after death. Death cancels a pre-existing hold and queued manual body inputs, without discarding world arrears. Summary/menu inspection does not stop clocks; lastSummary prevents reopening the same death repeatedly. Explicit next-story first drains the old observer's elapsed world debt up to its request timestamp, then changes once with no simulation tick and starts a fresh manual idle interval. Fast observation still stops at its current ending; full live-person switching, continued settled hero activities and revised year/death fast-run budgets remain OBS-01.2/LIFE-01/OBS-01.4, not completed by this foundation.


### actor-person.v1 — phase-owned detailed/NPC projections (stage68)
The current active hero owns its detailed physical state. Every other detailed actor follows its real canonical NPC owner; synchronization before/after existing world/actor phases is bookkeeping, not another action. A saved actorLink.version1/health/inventory records the last NPC-health projection and the same carried inventory reference. Restore reattaches originals without resource payments or a healing action. Selected hero sync skips dormant ownership; final death explicitly writes the transferred zero wallet and final needs before dormant synchronization can read stale values.
One-time old settled migration combines the real old legacy purse and later NPC wages, never adds the obsolete hero snapshot as a third purse. Existing explicit remaining keepsakes determine availability; a missing legacy list preserves known items instead of destroying them. Refreshable legacy views point at the same current wallet and original item objects. takeLegacyMoney/item removes from that actual owner once. Person-ID cannot be relabeled to transfer an item. Alive-body statistics are not initialized or healed on reload. NPC-health deltas map into detailed HP without repeated percentage rounding; all current UI HP remains rounded for reading.
actorLink.inventory is reattached to detailed inventory. Shared finite-stock consumption may open one carried ration into4 food units; it consumes the requested units and leaves surplus in real roadSupplies before touching freight. Insufficient combined stock changes nothing. Existing supply/needs/role-care switches guard their own consumption; ordinary people without this link retain previous stock semantics. Medicine is not fabricated by converting potions.
Inactive personPosition contains the actual NPC area/x/status, including null area for abstract legacy passage. Compatibility hero.area retains the last drawable scene until a real location exists; subsequent observer switching must use actual presence and cannot treat that view as a teleport. Opened food remains owned roadSupplies and must be admitted by active-hero food actions before free live-return UI. Full profession/brain handoff, person-keyed legacy gift limits, return UI and N-01 remain later tasks.

### npc-observer.v1 — stage71, OBS-01.2c.2a
An ordinary observed NPC keeps its real NPC controller, task, needs, wallet and inventory. Profile initialization is sandboxed and consumes no shared RNG, person ID, food, medicine, money, ability or actual parents. Unknown age remains unknown. Negative actorGeneration/nextNPCObservation is a private profile namespace and never advances nextGeneration or changes future adventurer inheritance. NPC job labels are lookup-only and are not added to the random protagonist profession pool.
projectNPCView derives presentation from actual work, travel and party ownership; it does not grant adventurer agency or a second action. Null-area legacy/group travel remains a view-only transit, and a real death in transit retains deathJourney/null area in its archive and milestones. Actual NPC death is recorded once without another die transfer, loot cache or grave. NPC profiles are excluded from selfDriven market sharing and witness prioritization. Party ownership remains mandatory with optional NPC arbitration disabled.
A single legacy adventurer retains its routing semantics when merely observing an NPC. Its old map is captured before an actual new adventurer birth; ordinary NPC knowledge is private metadata and does not mark global area discovery. Internal observeExistingPerson is zero-tick and rejects dead/child/missing or actually unlocatable targets. Public known-person selection, clock handoff and fresh input are the following .2c.2b stage; this preparation does not complete LIFE-01.

### observer-choice.v1 — stage72, OBS-01.2c.2b
observerKnowledge belongs to player observation, not to any body's planner. It records prior observed people, the current body's known people and actually visible local contacts. It never imports other actors' private contacts or supplies remote live coordinates; rows expose only a current local presence or a recorded last encounter. Actual unavailable/dead/child targets are omitted and revalidated at commit.
observerRequest captures source person, observation sequence and wall-time target. The adapter drains world arrears to that target, then commits at zero ticks; stale requests and targets who died during catch-up fail without an automatic substitute. Successful commit resets the wall clock/manual idle interval, closes old input/fast activity and selects the canonical journal/body. Creating a new adventurer while another lives is an explicit separate request. Previously observed bodies continue through their own existing controller.
A persistent delegated fresh-choice binding validates both the pressed row and its fresh release. Dragging, switching rows, cancellation, blur and viewport exit cannot select. Viewing a chooser does not pause world time. LIFE-01 remains responsible for replacing historical settled endings; this UI does not make them a fully simulated resident lifestyle.

### life.v1 — continuing residence (stage73, LIFE-01.1)
A new settlement decision creates a resident lifestyle, not a terminal life. The same detailed actor continues needs/work/relationships/obligations through its existing planner. It keeps party, wallet, inventory, career, title and purchase credit; no death hook, archive, NPC-controller handoff or automatic successor occurs. Home is a place preference, not a free deed or workshop. Known reachable workspaces use the existing owner/paid lease checks; missing premises permit ordinary wage labour. Work/quiet time can be interrupted by needs, existing commitments and periodic other decisions.
An actually empty local food stock can cause a trip to another known stocked settlement. The former home, mastery and title remain; work cannot immediately force a return to the same recorded shortage. Lifestyle-off preserves these facts and releases the special planning preference. Genuine later death still archives/transfers belongings once. Existing legacy ended settled snapshots remain historical until LIFE-01.3; the new settle API does not restart them. Finite individual client orders are LIFE-01.2, not part of this stage.
Operating-forge hazards are world actions: all actual self-driven workers are considered, and an NPC uses its actual controller. Workshop changes update each physically present detailed witness at its own actorView, once per event. log(...,{relay:false}) suppresses only duplicate cross-actor forwarding during such a broadcast, not the witness's own memory. Remote/transit actors do not receive the event; a dead observer receives only an afterlife world observation.

### service-repair.v1 — stage74, LIFE-01.2
The first client job uses an existing damaged workshop, its real NPC owner and a locally visible resident craft/time actor. It is optional work-orders content, not another body controller or an item generator. World.workOrders/nextWorkOrder initialize neutrally in v1 saves; at most80 finished records are retained while open ones remain.
service.contact.v1: the free personal NPC controller chooses whether to approach a real visible qualified body. An existing transport/party/migration role cannot become a commission through disabled optional arbitration. Only an actual same-area meeting within9 units creates a known quote; approaching does not tell the helper remotely or spend coins. A quote can be declined, missed, delayed by other obligations or made obsolete by the NPC's own repair.
service.escrow.v1: actual local acceptance reserves24 gold from the client wallet while leaving at least6 for needs. Its escrow remains separate from both bodies' spendable wallets. Wallets+treasury+escrow conserve the initial sum. Cancellation/deadline/death/off returns only unspent money; the same already paid material and partial work remain in the building. Core world time and all other actor plans continue.
repair.funding.v1: permission is bound to helper person-ID, the goal's service-ID, the specific workshop/area/owner and its damage stamp. The service's18 material coins enter the real local treasury and one finite civic tool is consumed once. Ordinary owner/credit repairs continue using their previous funding. A funded service reserves the property against simultaneous sale/gift/credit or another repair; it does not convey a title.
service.delivery.v1: only the real restoration proof with the contracted actor, client payer, target, damage and confirmed material payment releases the remaining6 fee once. The helper receives actual work/experience and a remembered client; relationship effects respect the relationship switch. An unrelated restore, wrong building or replay cannot claim the fee. A surviving client can then use or rent the same intact workshop under existing access rules. Current UI distinguishes the client's spendable wallet from its reserved funds and lists the helper's own jobs.

### legacy-continuation.v1 — stage75, LIFE-01.3
Only an ended settled snapshot with the same real legacy person is eligible. The current canonical NPC body, purse, controller, needs, current task and actual belongings win over its historical archive. The same person-ID, born, actorGeneration and real birth counter remain; no real RNG, time, people, deeds, wages, past work or healing are generated. Missing presentation fields use the isolated NPC profile builder. Legacy HP retains its original maximum and scaled wound delta. Current NPC employment is separate from the old adventurer career.
A recorded compact journey or actual group leader supplies position; an unknown last drawable area does not become physical presence or a selectable target. The old personal atlas remains private. Only factual NPC notes are added for the intervening period, without reconstructing missing years. Actual partial client repair, opened food portions and previous gift receipts remain authoritative.
Historical settled archives remain byte-for-byte snapshots. New archive keys are person/outcome/end, allowing a later real death of the same original generation. The NPC death archive uses the actual recorded date and cause; a late report records receipt time and labels the current estate balance. Missing death date/age/duration stays unknown rather than being inferred from hunger or the old settlement. Repeat restore/world ticks cannot duplicate a grave, archive, loot, birth or death entry. A dead client cannot learn a refund notification. This migration does not replace the newer active resident controller or implement resurrection.

### common-planning.v1 — stage76, LIFE-01.4
Detailed actors retain one existing controller/planner. Historical life.phase and nextOuting cannot admit, forbid or delay work, service jobs, travel or ordinary decisions. A remembered established home has a since record to avoid repeating its founding; it is neither a deed nor a planner mode. Old metadata remains compatible. Legacy NPCs from stage75 retain their actual compact controller.
Common local shortage handling, existing promises/preparation/story decisions, actual client repair, purchase/repair/access, paid work, trade, contacts and exploration share the ordinary priority path. A hungry place can motivate departure independently of a home flag; its recorded shortage prevents immediate return for employment/property alone. Homebody remains a personality preference, not a permission barrier.
workMotive is a pure assessment of the actual wallet against a finite living buffer, reachable remembered purchase, current gold destiny, explicit mastery target or existing civic food/medicine shortage with a matching real work effect. Cooldown releases the planner instead of automatically creating another leisure job. A wealthy supplied skilled person does not earn solely for work-counter growth. Actual workspace/lease checks apply to any repair worker, including travellers. More complex goals and materials remain I-01/LIFE-01.5/FORGE.
Quiet time is a bounded twelve-tick goal, available by preference even if paid work is off. A snapshot of personal known obligations, quoted orders, property knowledge, contacts/news, wallet and local shortage invalidates it when those inputs change. It creates one truthful decision record on changed circumstances, not invented neighbour events every reconsideration. An actual paid service needs no residence marker or lifecycle switch; canonical workOrders owns its agreement. Existing legacy residence.service fields may clear on close but no new agreement writes them. Acceptance supplies a real talk pose.

### service-materials.v1 — stage77, LIFE-01.5a
work-materials owns a bounded personal32-place stock cache and canonical helper.workMaterials.cargo, with a revision used by quiet-time reconsideration. Supplier availability/price is learned only by a live local body near the warehouse, never by reading a remote discovered area's current stock. Routes use the person's own known paths/avoidance; a source quote can be stale. Visiting another stocked warehouse can create an alternative naturally. v1 saves acquire empty records without replaying past visits, RNG, goods or time.
A real client may quote a damaged workshop with no local tool. With no known alternative the helper declines before reserving funds; an accepted job whose stock later disappears can wait, look elsewhere, negotiate once in an actual client meeting or cancel. Duty/hunger can make the client refuse an extension. No automatic supplier, guaranteed success, delegated helper or endless deadline is created. Ordinary preparation still forecasts energy/health/mana for the actual trip.
service.purchase.v1: an active qualified agreement binds helper, current physical supplier/position, original property/owner/damage and goal/service/source. One existing civic.tools unit is exchanged for3coins from its existing material escrow into that supplier's treasury. The sole tool instance belongs to the client and physically remains on the same canonical helper. toolPurchase is a receipt, not another stock. Replay, missing stock, competing helpers or disabled purchase dependencies cannot debit/create again.
repair.funding.v1 additionally accepts hasRepairTool and a funding receipt {payer,cost,advance,toolProvided}. The exact valid carried tool is consumed once; the remaining15 enter the destination treasury. Aggregate confirmed material payment remains18 and the original fee6 is paid once. Without a trip the original18/local-tool path is unchanged. A new local tool does not cause a second unit to be consumed when the paid parcel is available. Wrong owner/property/damage/goal/receipt cannot claim the parcel or fee.
Turning material travel off prevents new purchases/trips while preserving already bought goods; an existing valid tool can still finish its agreed repair. Other service dependencies retain their cancellation rules. Cancellation/death/deadline refunds only unspent money; the already bought tool remains client-owned on the actual living/dead holder. It is not an heirloom or free wallet balance. An actual death archives the historical cargo snapshot once and leaves the real instance on the canonical body; historical snapshots/receipts are not physical units. Full retrieval, delegation, inventory capacity, production and broader FORGE remain future contracts.
### personal-aid.v1 — stage78, LIFE-01.5b
A wounded living adult under the personal NPC controller may prefer an available local clinic or seek a physically visible helper / personally remembered medical contact over known roads. A remembered address is not a remote live location. Existing transport, party and other service ownership remains authoritative even when optional arbitration is disabled. Approach, request and medicine each use normal actions; needs continue.
The actual helper weighs health, energy, hunger, generosity, trust, obligations and the remaining paid deadline. Healing knowledge comes from a current medical profession or a genuinely recorded former rank; carrying herbs alone grants no skill. One owned herb with knowledge, or one owned potion, is consumed for actual HP and effort. Requests are bound to patient/helper IDs and their nonce; absent, dead, zero-HP, reassigned or expired patients cannot receive the action. No ailment removal, resurrection, currency reward or profession change is implied.
Medical contacts and relationships record actual meetings only. Optional-off preserves existing memories but permits no new treatment. Existing paid orders and client-owned cargo remain real; the common planner resumes or misses the actual deadline. Already installed materials and partial work survive cancellation; only unused escrow is returned. Observer switching, serialization and replay must preserve these physical outcomes.

### interior-place.v1 — stage79, IN-01.1
An optional tavern uses one existing proprietor and an existing facade in a root settlement. Seeded naming/presence consumes no simulation RNG. A persistent hall is a non-town leaf of the same physical map, with a door edge and no generated residents, civic treasury, medicine, food or frontier. Empty initialization projections advance only if no other areas are pending. Repeated initialization and v1 restore preserve identity and stock.
Door passage uses the actual entrance coordinate and one normal transition action. Entering requires an intact, open establishment during its hours with its living owner locally available; exiting to the same entrance remains possible after closure, death, absence or optional-off. Ordinary exploration does not repeatedly target closed rooms. The common planner, world clocks, body needs, inventories and canonical controllers remain authoritative. Critical needs lead outside; a field camp cannot be pitched inside a hall.
Free personal NPCs physically approach the entrance, enter and remain for bounded time, then leave. Transport/party/order claims cannot become a second tavern controller. Social relief requires another actual nearby person. No salary or new resources are awarded for standing in a hall. Rendering is sheltered and uses a local lamp rather than outdoor weather or sun/moon. Paid performances, service budgets and room booking are the following IN-01.2/IN-02.1 stages, not implied by the first hall.

### hall-activity.v1 — stage80, IN-01.2
Song work requires a real tavern hall, an actual capable performer, a physically present living payer, consenting nearby listeners and nine existing coins. Hunger, sleep, an incompatible task, controller ownership, sociability and enabled relationships affect consent. A closed, absent, empty or unfunded venue selects other work; a legacy street-song goal cannot mint a payment or a herb bonus.
At the actual performance point, one accepted nonce locks the venue and moves nine coins from the payer into finite held funds. Each of four normal actions costs energy; acceptance and song progress cannot execute twice in one tick. Only matching performer/venue/nonce and a surviving present audience release the held fee once. No generic job pay, faction bonus or random herb is added on this path. Completion gives ordinary work experience and real encounters.
World maintenance checks actual position, danger, life, audience, venue, expiry and rule switches. Interruption refunds only unspent held money once, including an estate when the original payer has died; it does not restore time or effort. NPCs continue their existing controllers and may leave. Serialization and choosing the payer as observer must preserve all balances and progress. Conversations use an actual nearby person's own known note or local weather, honor relationship settings and have a per-person cooldown. Wider TALK/REL and room services remain separate.

### room-booking.v1 — stage81, IN-02.1
Each existing tavern can own one persistent non-town guest room connected to its hall at90/8. The same exterior building and proprietor remain canonical. Creation draws no RNG and grants no residents, treasury, forage or items; an assigned missing room is not regenerated. Empty initialization counters advance only past the new already-empty projection.
One actual hall meeting reserves the room for240 absolute world ticks, capacity2, price3 from the payer's own purse to the existing host, leaving two coins for needs. Typed goal/building/location/contact and living adult payer are checked again. One room has one booking nonce; repeated receipt use does not charge or count a new rental. Other funded bodies cannot acquire its occupied capacity. Original occupants keep paid access during the period even when the owner dies or closes new admissions, provided the building remains intact and the module is enabled. No right is fabricated after expiry or for an added unlisted follower.
Human party members consent according to their actual loyalty, ethics and needs. An unwilling or oversized group chooses another common refuge; this stage does not split a party or create a second controller. A free personal NPC may book and walk through the same doors with its own money and needs. Normal transport/party/order claims remain authoritative. Sleep, food, medicine, danger and other plans still occur.
Movement, recovery and world time use normal actions. Room rest restores energy and gradual HP without curing conditions or replenishing inventory; hunger can interrupt it. Expiry, off, damage or death cannot lock an existing occupant inside. Data and existing world objects remain through reload and absence. Paid access is a delivered time-limited right, not another refundable repair-material escrow. Other institutions, furniture/storage ownership, HOME/C-01 and separated parties remain future work.

### pose-view.v1 — completed one-shot events (stage82, V-05.3)
DeathMotion is renderer-owned memory, never simulation state or RNG. A continuous observed alive→dead transition of the same person/birth with a current death date can start one240ms fall. Further ticks preserve its deadline and final pose. Loading, skipped history, a different observer or an already dead initial state uses the final pose immediately. A later actual death date after a living interval can start a new fall; this support does not implement resurrection. Disabled/reduced motion settles the event permanently. Rotation direction uses the recorded logical death position, so resizing cannot flip the body.
Short action effects require a new continuous observed world action. Replacing/loading a state, skipped ticks, old pickups and re-enabling a settled effect do not manufacture another action phase. Repeated actual living attacks retain their own new phases. Item taken flags and physical outcomes remain authoritative; drawing never clears them or writes rewards. Existing creature death tracks already retain final forms. Weather and an existing lit campfire remain continuous visual effects; there is no implemented persistent extinguished-campfire state to claim fixed here. World/auto/offline time and explicit successor controls keep their existing behavior.


camp-environment.v1 (расширение camp.v1, этап84): areaEnvironment(s, area) — чистое разрешение цепи temporaryCamp.parent до текущего канонического окружения. Нет снимка биома или нового поля save. Calendar/climate/weather/scene profile/composition читают родителя, Scene отдельно сохраняет физическую area для объектов/тел/костра. Scenery/biomePalette получают terrain ID/name/paletteVersion родителя, поэтому базовый пейзаж совпадает и реагирует на изменения мира. Camp не становится поселением/лесом и не получает population/civic/forage. Weather energy/speed действуют как снаружи; один костёр не превращает наземную стоянку в shelter. Подземный parent убирает небесный свет/осадки, костёр остаётся реальным источником. Missing/cyclic parent — ограниченный нейтральный fallback без создания area/людей/сырья. Переключение fieldcamp не стирает окружение существующей стоянки и оставляет прежний безопасный выход. Renderer никогда не продвигает тик/RNG и не переносит людей родителя.

scene-camera.v1 / CAM-01.1 (этап85): ограниченная проба одной короткой area a0 при type=road, default2.5°, конечный visual override cameraSlopeDegrees ограничен±4. Другие типы, interiors и transit имеют нулевую камеру; походный лагерь использует parent environment. Это художественная affine-проекция y'=y+(x−width/2)tan(angle), не полноценная3D/длинный маршрут. Canvas CSS не поворачивается. Опора земли, архитектура, тела, fixtures/источники и свет используют один matrix; перед физическим расчётом тени cameraLocalMatrix удаляет только глобальную проекцию, сохраняя реальный локальный scale существа. Точки тени проектируются ровно один раз. Нулевая геометрия identity, низ поверхности расширен на необходимый вынос без пустых нижних углов. Надголовный свежий DOM-эффект следует проектируемой точке; прикреплённые к HUD значки/подписи/дневник/чаршит не наклоняются. Renderer не меняет saved x/дистанцию/тик/вещи/RNG; выключение camera-probe возвращает ноль, reduced motion убирает движение, статичный ракурс сохраняется. Длинные сцены/сопровождение остаются CAM-01.2/.3.

area-length.v1 / CAM-01.2, этап86: сохранённые x0..100 остаются долей одной area. geometry.areaScale даёт default1 для отсутствующей/нечисловой/неположительной длины, положительная конечная длина ограничена50..600единицами; physicalDistance/areaOffset/areaStep едины для шагов, радиусов и относительных размещений. Новый необязательный профиль roadProfile=long-tract,length260 создаётся только addArea для новых road глубин2,6,10,..., без нового RNG и дополнительных объектов; старые области не меняются при restore. long-roads=false запрещает новые профили, сохраняя уже существующую геометрию и поездки. Обычные NPC сохраняют свою прежнюю8-тиковую каденцию, но6единиц — физические, а не6процентов. Герой/патрули/близкие появления/контакты/относительные party-позиции и forecast используют одну меру. Потребление сил/нужд идёт настоящими действиями. Public journey/freight сохраняют прежнюю роль и pace, длительность нового участка масштабируется его длиной; сохранённый segmentTicks/remaining посреди участка не пересчитывается. Прежние абстрактные служба/прибывания/караваны получают взвешенный бюджет, не выданы за новых поштучных ходоков. Граф знания/основные мотивы выбора прежние, оптимальность всех маршрутов не обещана.

scene-camera.v1 расширение86: sceneFrame строит полный world span из length и viewport, world coordinates объектов прежние. Pixel buffer/SceneGrade выделяется только под видимый viewport. CameraTrack — view-only сглаживание90ms в существующем RAF, строго0..maxScroll. При входе, замене save/наблюдателя, пропущенных тиках и resize вид сразу согласован с настоящей позицией; off/reduced снимают сглаживание. Единая affine-проекция включает горизонтальное сопровождение и небольшой ракурс. cameraLocalMatrix удаляет глобальные translation/slope перед расчётом физического caster, тень получает их один раз при рисовании; местные scale сохранены. Текст/дневник/листы неподвижны, свежий эффект получает актуальную проекцию из того же view. Camp остаётся короткой pocket-сценой с parent environment, transit имеет свой короткий вид. Ни render, ни resize, ни off не меняют физический путь или часы. Spatial anchor переиспользуемого camp для нескольких тел отдельно передан во входящие, не объявлен решённым этим расширением.

CAM-01.3 / этап87: forecast движения теперь учитывает тот же допуск достижения1физическаяединица, что engine.move/goal arrival. Это не ускорение пути: фактическое движение осталось прежним; дождь/снег на длинном участке больше не завышают число нужных шагов на один. Новый regression road-forecast.test.js прогоняет реальную погоду/движение в обе стороны. Scene.draw публикует только DOM renderMs — CPU подготовки команд текущего кадра, включая его view callback, без GPU completion/engine input time; это не поле мира/сохранения/новый таймер.

### combat-judgment.v1 — stage90, H-03.2a

The existing fight controller chooses one existing action. A lethal reply remains a fact independently of courage. A brave finishing attempt can still fail; a remembered heavy hit from this opponent changes willingness to gamble. A known rescue deadline may justify more risk. No future RNG is sampled and no enemy is weakened.

One ordinary retreat roll retains its previous probability. If blocked, the same existing turn may use one available remedy, spell or attack. It cannot attempt another free escape. Time magic skips only the reply specified by the existing spell; it does not supply health or guarantee a safe journey. An ineffective potion is not treated as certain survival. Disable restores the old selection/RNG sequence while retaining saved observations.

Each canonical actor owns bounded combatJudgment.last/lastReply/lastHit. last.attackReply means the possible response to an ordinary weapon attack; last.selectedReply describes the explicitly chosen alternative, or null for legacy/escape. Requested and performed actions are distinct fields. Costs are recorded after the actual action, before any separate enemy drain. This metadata gives no XP or resources. v1 saves need no reset.

### response-range.v1

Pure tacticalResponseValue, mutationResponseValue and combatProtection compose four possible base attack rolls, current visible phase, local pack and the hero's own defenses. Mirror echo can strike even during a nominal preparation phase. Healing changes duelist thresholds; weapon damage can awaken fury. Halfling avoidance is a probability. The range concerns the next enemy reply, not all later dangers or disease impulses.

Outgoing damage uses learned resistance rates when present. An untested resistance remains a range across world rules: the forecaster does not read this enemy's hidden elemental rate. Miss/phase effects prevent a false promise of a guaranteed finishing hit. The provider never mutates battle state, records item memories or draws RNG. Existing actual response effects remain authoritative.

### spell-availability.v1

availableCombatSpells is pure. It returns known affordable spells whose cooldown has elapsed, with snapshot manaCost/cooldownTicks/effectPower under current flags and owned time enchantment. castInCombat accepts an optional exact available spell; an unavailable request spends nothing. The ordinary three-argument call retains its prior priority. Actual effects, one payment, cooldown, pacification probability and reply rules are unchanged. COM-01 may add other actions later; this stage does not invent a block, new spell or resource.

### silhouette.v1

The actual Scene.paintPerson commands are recorded once and replayed for the visible body. Opaque filled paths and stroke segments supply separate contour polygons: no bounding box, convex hull or independently authored costume mask. Walking feet, hands, cape, headwear and species head use the current pose and appearance. The shared paintRoof path also supplies the cached house shell, including overhang and chimney.

Each contour has support, local height and a small finite depth (2 for bodies, 5 for houses) in the existing 2.5D scene. Front/back and edge faces project onto the existing receiving surface, clipped at its boundary. Depth prevents a lamp on the same support line from reducing the shadow to an invisible ray. Low sources have bounded lengths; this is a finite artistic model, not full 3D physics. Current outer rotation, including a fallen body, is applied before height projection. The camera is removed once from local transforms and applied once to the result.

Actor command caches hold at most128 entries per scene; static shells at most64. No simulation state, RNG, event or second animation loop is created. Disabling retains the former simple casting path. V-07.1 deliberately retains the existing sky plus nearest local source policy and existing budgets. V-07.2 separately handles all contributing sources and refined occlusion/lighting.

### light-contribution.v1

source-shadows keeps the complete registry of actual active fixtures, fire, portals, blessing and the same rendered charm glow. The legacy LIGHT_BUDGET=3 is a legacy selection limit, not permission to light a fourth object while omitting its shadow contribution. Disabled lighting still returns no sources. Disabled source-shadows restores the former registry, source profiles and sky/nearest-local projection policy.

Each current caster evaluates every source by coverage and three height samples through the finite depth volume of existing opaque house shells. Source and caster support depths are distinct: a rear facade does not block a ray travelling wholly along the foreground. An emitter inside its own shell may shine outward. This selected architecture model is not full 3D or global ray tracing; trees, furniture and all creatures are not newly ray-traced. A source can be outside-range, occluded, inactive, an own-emitter, or have no-ground-reach. Each rejection has an explicit geometric condition.

The physical projection uses source height above its actual foot. A low flame cannot project an upper body point onto the floor; those contour portions are clipped at the light plane. Local perspective scales each point rather than shearing the whole body by one number. The floor receiving disk has radius sqrt(range^2-height^2), centered at the light foot, separately from the visible airborne glow. Taller street/wall source profiles have matched radii. Projections are finite and clipped by receiving surface and reach. Source-off retains the prior projection formula.

Available light at the caster normalizes per-source shadow loss; combined alpha is at most0.4 before spatial falloff. Own emission may fill other incoming shadows but never self-casts. Sun, moon, compact lamps and diffuse fire/magic have different edge softness; weather weakens and softens sky shadows. Ambient contact comes from the actual lower foot contours and retains gaps, not an unrelated common ellipse.

The existing caster budget36 and animation loop remain. Complete source registration is separate from viewport/influence culling. Actor command cache128, static shells64, world-contour and projected-contour caches64 each; current shape/support/source/surface/physical-policy keys invalidate only the relevant projection. No world RNG, event, item, motion or second simulation action is produced. Cache and visibility facts are view data only.

### intention.v1 / canonical affairs

knownAffairs returns ephemeral domain/id references to active quests, a personal commitment, locally delivered medical requests and own/party needs. It does not copy them into another quest registry. The first adapter handles witnessed assault over the existing calm planner; it is not a claim to finish general I-01.2 utility/hysteresis, amnesia or ordinary NPC affairs. One hero keeps at most one current intervention, one suspended executor, one last result and12 recent IDs. A full four-quest portfolio cannot suppress a situation requiring no new contract or reward.

Visible care, courage, current resources and a bounded existing combat forecast select fight, warning, available local guard or refusal without future RNG. Own urgent needs and near-expiring known obligations may take priority. A newly recruited guard is already responding to the same matter; the hero does not immediately undo that agreement with another warning unless the guard becomes critically wounded. Talking is an ordinary local action; fight remains hunt and uses normal preparation, combat costs, replies, loot and possible death.

### danger-contact.v1

Actual orders replies and public-journey collisions stamp one mechanical record on the same person. The record is not fabricated when the hero gathers, and the decision system does not read view-only actorFacts. Matching local bodies and a live local enemy must be within30 physical units; the last observed attack is fresh for24ticks. Wounds are used as local severity bands. No hidden victim address, remote death or resistance is learned. Other assault/controller domains are not silently declared covered. Out-of-sight outcome is unknown, not automatically rescued.

### safety-advice.v1

One spoken warning within9units spends the hero's action and1energy. A pending message does not itself move, heal or stop the recipient. At a later orders action, condition/duty/trust can accept or decline. Accepted retreat is selected before watchNeeds can spend a medicine/meal action; upkeep remains once and the normal enemy response still occurs. The person can die. Repeating a warning does not add an action. Module-off preserves the message, which may become stale rather than delivering a fresh command on re-enable.

### local-guard-order.v1

An existing visible free resident professional guard in the same civic area must be healthy, rested and not starving or already controlled by another activity. Consent compares that person's capability and duty with the visible enemy; refusal is possible. No person, supplies, health or purse is created. A missing old attack field derives from the guard's existing work; an explicit zero is never upgraded. The same guard receives an ordinary orders assignment. Its current actionControl is retained, so a personal action cannot be followed by an orders attack in the same tick. Accepted local orders finish at the actual location without the legacy return-to51 teleport and continue as a real obligation when new interventions are disabled.

### suspended-action.v1

The existing goal object moves into one suspended slot with its remaining steps; actual items and promises stay in their original domains. A warning or request does not also gather in the same tick. Return checks active canonical quest state, already fulfilled needs, remembered avoidance and known routes. Fresh stock is read only locally; remote depletion is discovered on arrival. Current preparation is checked again after resuming. Existing aid/property/service/quest validators remain authoritative. Disabling can restore the old executor but cannot cancel an ongoing combat for a free escape, erase cargo, repeat a reward or turn a declined quest into a new copy. Broader re-evaluation of every kind of affair remains I-01.2 and its domain contracts.


### outfit-visual.v1 (этап101)

itemOutfit — замороженный stateless v1 рецепт по item.id/base, без RNG/мутации legacyвещи. Только equipment.armor меняет garmentId/coat/material/cloak/trim/plateColor/edgeColor, сумка не влияет; существующий packSize честно зависит от поклажи. Два цветовых поля и крупная форма (длинный плащ/мантия/нагрудник) читаются в Scene и портретном воротнике. Натуральный capColor грибного народа берётся до окраски одежды, skin/hair/culture не меняются. NPCбез detailedтела читает старый fallback; detailedNPC/попутчик/отпущенный adventurer читает тот же фактический комплект, без нового меча по должности. Старые ролевые props не превращены в материальный предмет.

Scene.paintPerson и portrait используют drawGarment; контур записывает те же команды, нового costume maskнет. Первое отображение не пишет visualполя в предмет, версию несёт фиксированный рецепт. После передачи оригинал удаляется у прежнего владельца существующим owner API, новый облик только после настоящего надевания. Новый archiveLife сохраняет outfitSnapshot до очистки; архив рисует свой портрет, не нынешнего героя. Старые архивы без снимка не регенерируются. Off нового модуля сохраняет вещи/снимки и возвращает прежние виды; appearance/equipmentoff не дарят и не уничтожают одежду. Ранг шляпы остаётся старым визуальным знаком и не создаёт новый экземпляр. Покупка/смена по погоде и баланс защитных свойств — V-02.4/GEAR, не этот слой.


### armor-tradeoff.v1 (этап102)

Mail/доспех — тяжёлый: +2к существующему item.power защиты и +0.4силы на наземное действие ходьбы, умножение на погоду общее. Coat/плащ — лёгкий: существующая защита, без дополнительной усталости. walkingEnergy читает физическое equipment и используется фактическим движением и estimatePlan; пассажирский sea/portal тик не считается пешим. Preparation key содержит тяжёлый item-ID, чтобы смена комплекта пересчитала известный бюджет.

legacyArmorDefense отключает прежнее h.armor*2, пока фактически надет armor-item; рейтинг не удаляется, после снятия вновь fallback. armorCoverage согласован для swarm/караванного ответа; реальный бой и incomingReply используют одинаковую базу/физический bonus. Route-memory учитывает реальную надетую защиту вместо второй legacyкопии. Уже одетому не планируется ещё одна покупка абстрактной кожаной брони.

armorChoice при фактической находке читает только известный путь через переданный ctx.knownRoute, максимум3сцены, геометрическую длину, собственные нужды и смелость. Неизвестная удалённая цель/будущие враги не сканируются. Это оценка приобретения, не полная экипировка запасного комплекта/бесплатный обход: GEAR-01.2 остаётся. Более тяжёлая находка может остаться в сумке, возможен отказ от первой при недостатке сил. Обычный receiveItem сохраняет заменённый экземпляр в сумке; старое переполнение/продажа сохраняют прежний договор и LOOT-01.3. Свойства текущего предмета видны в листе, известные эффекты/оценка снимком в прежней loot-result записи, без дополнительной реплики/RNG. Off нового модуля возвращает точные старые числа/выбор/часы и не стирает предметы.


### work-loss.v1 (этап103)

Repair work records the actually leased/owned workshop in hero.workLife.last; normal planned repair has an explicit goal.workplace binding. Local access denial observes that binding, not an arbitrary profession label. The property observer also retains a visibly closed workplace when its owner left, and marks a physically absent bound place missing locally. Remote workshop/owner/health state is never polled as closure knowledge. Last job's later remembered non-intact observation can produce one pending loss; repeat observations before new actual work cannot reissue it. Later career change supersedes that old job instead of forcing a return.

After existing body/party needs and urgent commitments, the pending cause compares canonical active rescue/medicine/herbs/supply promises to known kin against connected known workshops. It uses existing questMeaning importance/urgency and bounded career/practice investment. Relatives/requests/rewards are not invented; planQuest and the existing job owner perform movement, cargo, payment and completion. Alternative access is validated/paid only at arrival. Chosen family/craft/open outcome is a record of reconsideration, not a second quest/controller. No option returns to ordinary planning, not a mandatory idle loop. Worker's existing gold/XP/rank/profession are not reset or refunded.

Prepared supply coverage retains the old owner's existing units/prices: buying removes8supplierstock/costs4coins, completion adds40sourcecivicstock; this is the pre-existing crate/settlement accounting, not a new claim of physical unit conservation or R-06/C-01 completion. Existing overflow/material/service systems retain authority. New module off preserves workLife but freezes its decisions and new bindings, and returns legacy access/planning. World time, observation, death and the explicit next-story button are unchanged.

### service-access.v1 / clinic-availability.v1 / employment-availability.v1 / merchant-stock.v1 / store-access.v1 (этап104)

Capability is a pure read of the existing service, not another NPC controller. Qualified living adult residents must actually be in this area and under their personal controller; enabled needs can make them unavailable. An absent or destroyed clinic entrance blocks clinical care, not neighbouring food or personal rest. Existing medicine doses and treatment owner remain authoritative. Natural rest and carried remedies require no employee.

Employment revalidates worker, room, enabled material requirements and existing treasury before rent or labour. Completed ordinary work debits its existing salary once from that treasury. Food payments reach the existing town, without initializing missing money to a gift. Temporary absence or lack of funds does not create a permanent R-01 workplace catastrophe. The earlier property/owner and material-order escrow remain authoritative.

The merchant's old aggregate stock partitions once into gear and medicine without increasing total units. Matching purchases consume one real unit; no remote, dead or travelling body buys. Arrival at a functioning supplier transfers up to the finite cap from actual tools/medicine, independently of ordinary supply production, as canonical quest cargo already does. No periodic free refill, new merchant purse or full economy is claimed. Existing quest crate units remain the old contract documented above; blocked supply promises keep their canonical executor and may resume after a witnessed reopening.

Only local witnessed capabilities enter bounded hero.memory.services (128 latest records). Planning reads those snapshots; unknown services may be checked, absence is learned on arrival, and old job-specific observations refresh locally. No global merchant-location knowledge is granted. New module off preserves its snapshots and stock but freezes these policies and restores published103 decisions; v1 saves require no reset. World clocks/observation/death controls are unchanged.

### craft-respect.v1 (этап105)

Directional person.craftRelations[subject] records only actual witnessed completed repair. An ordinary completed repair wage job or the canonical workshop restoration history is its source. Local contact uses real actor/party position, not the selected observer or stale home; sleeping/dead/absent/distant people do not witness. A monotonic last work tick/event ID per pair prevents repeated or older replay awards. Each new finished action adds6 respect, bounded100, without copying personal trust. The existing controller, labour, salary, tools, property and escrow stay authoritative; no new lesson, occupation or reward generator.

A critical repair offer requires the old real skill/rank conditions and the commissioner's personally known respect>=6. High affection alone is insufficient; negative personal trust does not prevent a professional quote. Its basis snapshots the witnessed event and distinct trust, and the quote explains the distinction. Existing voluntary recruitment still checks its own trust, not respect. Work contracts reserve and pay exactly the existing funds; no free companion or property transfer.

An actually accepted unchanged repair whose known deadline expired reduces the informed client's existing agenda.bonds toward the helper by4, once, preserving respect. Unaccepted offers, dead helper/client, disabled work rules or changed property terms do not blame the helper. Knowledge is local to the informed client; it does not magically update the helper's opinion of the client. The relations sheet shows the observed person's own known craft judgments alongside its distinct personal trust, with unknown trust labelled honestly. Switching off craft-respect or relationships preserves records and restores old hiring; old saves need no reset. Further lessons, fear and group affinity remain separate future stages.

### craft-practice.v1 (этап106)

Canonical person.skillPractice.craft counts only finished physical workshop restorations, from their actual actor/payer/damage history. A property/damage episode is one operation; monotonic completion tick rejects repeated/older replay. The last16 causes remain, lifetime total is separate. Ordinary wage-job labels, rank, reading old logs and unfinished repairs give no practice; imported careers receive no invented retroactive operations.

Three operations make a self-study source available to interested healthy craft/time profiles. Known connected intact benches are considered, existing workspaceAccess revalidates ownership/actual owner and takes its real rent once if borrowed. Three study actions each cost2energy, no salary/XP/tool gift; partial study persists after interruption/save, an unavailable bench gives no free method. Existing commitments, bodily needs and accepted jobs keep earlier priority. An uninterested traveller may continue ordinary activities. The learned fit-working-part method remains canonical to this person through profile changes.

During a valid paid physical restoration, fitting uses the original material already funded by the existing repair owner (18coins and one tool). One repair.part provenance marks that material as fitted, removes one labour action once, then the ordinary repair owner finishes and pays any existing fee once. Total labour becomes2 instead of3; no extra tool, inventory original, refund or property title appears. workshop.lastFittedPart is a historical record of that part/damage episode, not a second loose item or a claim that a later burned building is intact. Missing real materials still prevent work. Previously executed effects remain when switched off; new records/study/fitting freeze, old money and profession remain. First source and application only: travelling lessons, other professions and independent skill tree remain future SK stages.

### causal-history.v1 (этап107)

hero.causalHistory keeps up to8 cause chains/16events per chain, preserving the origin and reporting dropped details. Its cause is the existing retreat memory ID and personal commitment, never a new quest or planner. Actual decision, completed wage work and successful weapon purchase join only through that explicit cause/goal.commitment. A known equipment target becoming satisfied is a separate prepared event and does not imply buying: loot or missing old detail remain possible. A later real encounter with the same original enemy/area after preparation can record return once; remote enemy updates and merely having a better weapon cannot.

Event IDs are stable cause/kind/tick, repeats do not reward or reissue actions. Only this metadata is added; no new logs, RNG, clocks, money, objectives or route choices. Reads return a copy. The detailed sheet/current journal offers in-sheet links to actual origin row IDs, which scroll without a simulation command or browser navigation. The main game gains no panel. Text and IDs escape before rendering. Old missing origins/actions remain explicit partial data; nearby journal sentences and private NPC facts are never used to fill gaps. Off/commitments-off preserves evidence and freezes new records. Native link checks must compare full exports with real automatic time, not assume menus pause the world.

### principles.v1 (этап108)

The first idea is verify-guilt. Its source is the existing mystery owner's actually revealed local false verdict, after canonical finishStory in that same tick. The hero must be alive, present, not in transit and own the case. Correct/unrevealed/remote/old/other-owner cases do not become personal news. A source stores case/event/time/known innocent identity, never culprit identity. Up to8 sources and a monotonic lastSourceTick prevent duplicate or old grants. No prior saved political/religious position is fabricated.

A pending idea may be rejected by the current temperament or considered in three actual reflection actions at1energy each. The personality condition is curiosity+generosity>=1.15; this is a deterministic policy, not a universal moral upgrade. Body needs, commitments, active investigations and accepted work keep earlier priority. Partial thought persists across interruption/save. No wage, rank or automatic pardon is granted. A new genuinely learned mistake may reopen consideration of a rejected idea; it is not the same source replayed.

An adopted principle changes only the existing mystery planner: after one clue, choose a further personally known reachable clue despite habitual impatience. It does not read the hidden culprit/reliability to choose, create a new case/witness, reveal unknown roads or guarantee a true verdict. A nearby visible enemy capable of at least half-current-HP damage, or HP<30%, can defer that extra check; ordinary danger/combat remains authoritative. The original evidence/trial owner still produces every clue, sentence, imprisonment and reward. The sheet shows accepted/rejected/pending and its known source. Off preserves idea/evidence and restores old mystery choices. Text/discussion sources, other principles and broader ideology remain future work.

### brace.v1 (этап109)

The actual charged windup response stamps the personally fighting body's warning with enemy-ID, area, battle turn and world tick. Its payload/key must agree; unknown, stale (>1world tick), other-turn/area, dead, travelling or energy<8 cases offer no brace. The existing combat-judgment owner adds the option; tactics and that decision system remain dependencies. At most32 warning records persist; switching observer neither teaches the other body nor clears the first body's source.

A selected brace spends the whole action and exactly8energy, with no outgoing/party attack roll, item, spell or free interruption. The original enemyResponse still performs its real charged roll, secondary conditions, theft, recovery and possible death. A same-tick/enemy-bound receipt prevents duplicate payment and gives no protection to another enemy/body or next tick. After ordinary protection/enchantment/race, received harm is multiplied by1-strength, rounded up with minimum1 on a landed hit (real racial dodge0 stays0). Strength uses the actual physical profile, might and worn/legacy-effective defense, capped65%. incomingReply uses the same final transform across its four base rolls. No original item, salary or ownership changes.

The cautious policy may choose guard against a known substantial charge when its forecast survives, rather than sacrificing its attack action. Another character keeps the original attack; exhausted actors fall back to existing retreat/care/last-stand logic. It is not promised always better than fleeing. The actor painter has one braced lowered/raised-arm pose, shared with its contour shadow; no shield is conjured from inventory. Off preserves warnings/receipts but restores old choices and damage. Separate learnt interruption/counterattack remain future work; death still stops body actions while world/auto/offline continue and successor remains explicit.

### delve-attempt.v1 / delve-reentry.v1 / expedition-preparation.v1 (этап110)

A body owns a monotonic attempt serial and at most24 records keyed by canonical dungeon root. The active delve carries its attempt-ID across hall transitions and reconstructed hunt goals. A pre-existing active v1 delve imports its real started tick with an explicit incomplete marker, zero invented earlier contacts and no fabricated new-start reward. A contact opens only at the actual fight hook; repeated same-tick hooks cannot duplicate its action count. A blocked escape remains that contact; successful escape closes it and pauses the same attempt. A real death marks its final outcome once, without stopping world time or creating a successor. Room completion alone retains ownership of treasure, XP and floor-cleared state.

A refusal stores the actor's own resources, ailments, known methods, available companions, relevant accepted promise and personally encountered enemy state. Passing40/600ticks is not a new reason. Meaningful healing/energy recovery, removal of a condition, stronger effective equipment, an actual remedy/new spell/ready companion or a new relevant promise permit reconsideration. Observed enemy progress requires a later personal warning; remote fresh HP does not count. Local absence is checked only while physically in that room. A courageous curious actor or a caring rescuer can consciously accept risk once per effective equipment/remedies/conditions/methods/party/promise signature. This scoped passage does not disable the actual combat judgment or prevent a later retreat/death. Failed preparation does not consume that consent. The last refusal and accepted-risk signature survive timeout and later attempts at the same root.

Admission and resumption use the existing known-route preparation estimate with actual energy, health, mana and party readiness. Insufficient resources may invoke existing field/paid rest, never a gifted antidote or a new rescue route. A synchronous justified-entry query temporarily projects only its own accepted passage and restores the original attempt/record in finally, including exceptions; it does not commit a start or advance RNG/time. Disabling preparation uses the existing85%HP/65energy entry threshold. Disabling delve-attempts preserves its memory and returns the published prior planner; delves-off leaves physical rooms and suspended records intact. Counts are observations, never progression rewards. General branching dungeon generation and broader expedition systems remain separate tasks.

New optional entry also needs a personal cause: a related accepted rescue/relic/scout promise, an unfinished hoard/relic destiny with sufficient willingness, or actual curiosity/courage. A caring actor's urgent unrelated rescue can postpone that optional start. The gate is not generated merely to force an uninterested person into a new adventure. The existing peaceful-career restriction remains; the policy does not turn every life into a peaceful or a dungeon life. Blessing/inspiration expiry is explicitly not ailment recovery.

The first imported canonical root is anchored in delve.attemptRoot. A legacy entry through a middle hall can have later old-style room.root values pointing at that middle entry; those values do not create a second expedition or replace its attempt-ID. The physical old root/rooms are retained.

### workshop-tool.v1 / tool-learning.v1 / tool-repair.v1 / tool-part.v1 (этап111)

The first concrete incident concerns one fixed clamp in an existing canonical workshop. A completed physically local repair wage job or the owner's artisan operation records use once per actor/world tick; its current receipt stays bounded. The fixture's existing-workshop origin is not a portable tool gift or added warehouse stock. Twenty-four productive operations break its guide once. The intact room/title remains; actual hero repair employment and the owning artisan's work/output stop. The observer remembers at most16 personally seen fixtures; repeated identical observation preserves a refusal signature instead of re-opening the same plan. The current working status is a small read-only module, separate from finance/repair/event dependencies; the teacher scheduling receipt likewise has no supplier/planner dependency. The static graph remains acyclic.

This first repair activity is for a real owner's own property. A renter can observe the fault and choose different employment; a second externally funded hired-repair contract registry is not introduced. The artisan owner spends3own coins and one local stock unit after three actual controlled work turns. Missing material or budget permits existing productive farm/study, preserving occupation and knowledge. Paid material already inside a transferred fixture is reusable and is not charged again. Maintenance-off suspends remaining special work while permitting former ordinary work; enabling may resume it.

An interested novice owner with a real idle local healthy personal-controller artisan can choose three lessons. Pupil and actual teacher each spend an action; the canonical teacher wallet receives exactly1coin for each performed lesson. Needs, other commitments, departure and control changes prevent teaching. The people's8tick scheduler can supply the same already claimed action receipt; an explicit local lesson on other ticks claims that person's ordinary controller once, not a cloned/free speaker. Completed paid lesson records survive interruption/save/profession change; three give one clamp method. Existing confirmed fit-working-part knowledge is also usable knowledge; no former work is retrospectively credited with practice/XP. The method permits one real local stock unit and3own coins to fund a guide, then two labour actions. Funded material/progress remain inside the fixture. The learner can earn money or choose a different activity if the next step becomes unavailable.

Another owner can instead use remembered warehouse snapshots and actual known roads, buy one standard guide from a real stock kit for3own coins, carry the uniquely identified original in the ordinary bag, return and install that one instance. Actual arrival rechecks provider/stock/budget/capacity. A stale source grants no part and charges nothing. It is a physical item with source and history, not an entry in a second virtual inventory; sale/death/heirloom transfer use existing owners. The base workPart classifier always keeps it out of equipment, even while maintenance is disabled. Installation retains its provenance inside the fixture and removes it from the bag once. No learning/visit/retry counter grants goods or money.

Known method, material/source knowledge, observed master availability and meaningful budget changes permit reconsideration. Time/seen timestamps alone do not. Unchanged failure can give other work without repeated repair attempts. The ordinary screen stays one page; the bag labels the original part as material with price/history, and known tool damage is distinguished from an intact building. The label uses personally remembered damage, not a remote fresh lookup.

Validation scope: prepared owned-title/prior-wear/resource/teacher variants establish both autonomous solutions, insufficient funds/material/master, actual teacher action/payment, partial learning, resumed production, stale sources, save/off/replay and original-part death transfer. Six natural4800world prefixes and three prepared200 continuations are distinct populations. In this natural sample no fixture reached24uses: zero faults/repairs/hero lessons is reported rather than tuning frequency to force an incident. Initial off comparison is against actual published110; an old engine interpreting new in-flight goal kinds is not claimed equivalent. The final UI-only property-label correction has a separate byte-identical165non-entry-asset proof plus compiled replay, retaining the actual original audit revision. Native320×740 exports are compared as whole saves with actual clocks. Wider tool types, outside-funded helper jobs and a whole skill tree remain separate work.
# REL-01.2 / этап112: отношения пары на привале

`party-relations.v1`, переключатель `party-relations`, использует camp-action/stock-transfer/npc-controller и канонических `people`. Собственное направленное чувство к другому спутнику хранится в `person.partyRelations[otherId]`, отдельно от trust/loyalty героя: диапазон −12…12, максимум12 партнёров и8 событий на партнёра. Источник — настоящая просьба о личной еде/лекарстве, с ID лагеря/наблюдаемого героя, участниками, местом, временем и реальным расходом; отсутствующие, спящие и не участвовавшие не получают эту память.

Одна просьба за настоящий привал: голодный без своей еды либо раненый без двух единиц личной медицины обращается к готовому бодрому соседу с достаточным запасом. При помощи прежний конечный запас расходуется через stock-transfer (еда1/медицина2), нужда уменьшается прежней величиной; медицина не воскрешает и не обещает вылечить любое состояние. Оба расходуют действие/силы. Необщительный, менее обязательный владелец вправе оставить свой запас себе; это отказ от действительной просьбы при существующем ресурсе, не обида за выдуманное отсутствие. Нет общего склада или телепатического события.

Приготовленный страж с живым соседом может предложить один необязательный общий дозор. Первая встреча пары или новая настоящая просьба даёт повод предложению; один переход к следующей ночи не повторяет его и не вытесняет хобби бесконечным дозором. lastWatch хранит рассмотренный источник отдельно от чувства. Неразрешённая обида любой стороны означает отказ именно от совместного занятия; оба остаются в отряде. Согласие расходует силы и снижает тревогу героя на3, без нового боевого бафа/XP/золота. Резервируется прежняя campSession; обычные спасательные разговоры имеют прежний приоритет.
